- **axe-core Accessibility Testing Engine for Automated Web UI Auditing**: axe-core is the industry-standard accessibility testing engine by Deque Systems that automatically detects WCAG 2.0, 2.1, and 2.2 violations in web interfaces. It integrates into any existing test framework — Playwright, Cypress, Selenium, Jest — to catch accessibility issues during development rather than after deployment. ([agentskillexchange/skills](https://github.com/agentskillexchange/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/axe-core-accessibility-testing-engine) · ★65)
- **axe-core-accessibility**: Specialist in Deque Axe-Core automated accessibility auditing, WCAG 2.1 AA/AAA compliance checks, color contrast calculation, and screen reader verification. ([sahil24raj/SR24plus-engineering-toolkit](https://github.com/sahil24raj/SR24plus-engineering-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/sr24-custom-engineering/axe-core-accessibility) · ★1)
